The Technology behind Artificial Intelligence and its Rapid Evolution
We can only imagine what goes on in the research and development departments of Netflix, Facebook, and Google, as they develop the most revolutionized AI technology available, using the most sophisticated data collecting systems that could help make all of this a reality in the future. But, to get there, we have to first know what it will involve as we take a ride on the road to AI.
Data collection
Artificial Intelligence has a high demand for data. It can easily be compared to the human brain as it takes in information by the ton from our environment and processes it to make conclusions and observations. AI is useless if it does not have the information it needs.
In the world of AI stack, this data is not limited to only one source. This is mainly due to the available data that is continuously received through the internet of things, where millions of technology devices all have a connection to each other. With the AI stack’s collection layer, this is the software that makes these connections possible, not only that but for services that are web-based which receive data from third-party suppliers and databases that contain information concerning social media API, weather, and news. With a VPA (Virtual Personal Assistant), they let the collection of data to happen via human speech. The speech will be converted to data by way of natural language recognition, regardless of commands directed towards machines or background noise.
Data Storage
After the data is collected or have streams set up to pour the information into a business in real-time, you must be able to put it somewhere. When it concerns AI data, we are talking about Big Data. This is because a big space for storage is needed as well as needing to be accessible in a short period of time. Normally, cloud technology will take the reins in this aspect. A few businesses use the resources and capability to create their own data centers for distribution. With different technologies like Spark or Hadoop, it can easily handle this much information. However, cloud infrastructure from a third party, like Microsoft Azure or Amazon Web Services, can easily provide solutions that are more suitable. Storage can be enlarged or minimized as needed which will save the business money as the platform will show many methods of integration.
Analytics and Data Processing
This might be the one thing that people will consider important while they talk about AI and if they do not have the complete stack, then the insights that they have will be too limited to work with.
With AI processing, there are so many types of technology that are included like recommendation engines, image recognition, deep learning, natural language processing, and machine learning. These topics are all the current hype that we hear all the time, while businesses are busy enthusiastically talking about how their technology is the best.
Many algorithms like these are sometimes used as a service that can be deployed via a private or public cloud or an API from third parties, Data Lake, private data center, or basic data collection.
With all of the capabilities, flexibility and power in the algorithms are what makes the difference between previous AI and the current AI along with the data increase that is available. We see the power increase today because of GPUs being deployed. These GPUs are the original processors designed to handle tasks that are heavy-duty and can generate computer visuals that are sophisticated. The prowess they possess allows them to be ideal data crunchers. Another processing unit that can easily handle related AI tasks must be able to provide a longer change in the performance of AI in the future.
Data Output and Reporting
If you plan to make the strategy of your AI to help machines become efficient and effective (for purposes of predictive maintenance or decreasing resource or power usage), then this technology will communicate every operational insight from the AI processing to all of the systems that it will be a benefit of. There are other ways that a human could easily act upon. These include having a sales assistant that use a handheld terminal that can interpret and read recommendations and insights that are related to the customers who may be standing ahead. We might see that other cases may design the output as a virtual personal assistant, graphics, charts, and dashboards.
Artificial Intelligence’s Rapid Evolution
Even though the idea of Artificial Intelligence is not a new term, it was during the mid-1950s that the concept was explored as an authentic possibility. With philosophers, mathematicians, and scientists working together on the same concept, a breakthrough did not occur until Alan Turing made a suggestion that if reason and information were available to make decisions and have problems solved, then who says machines do not have the ability as well? Even though Turing mentioned how to test a machine and its intelligence in a paper in 1950, his work was not able to advance past that.
The reason for it was that there was an issue with the computers of the time. Prior to any new growth in technology, the computers required a major upgrade. Although the computers executed all of the commands, they had no way of storing the information. This was unfortunate because the loss of funding played a major role in the halt up until the mid-70s.
So it wasn’t until 1974 that saw the flourishing of computers. They became able to store data and all sorts of information, they were affordable and they were faster than ever. There were many demonstrations that also began to show promise. This included the General Problem Solver developed by Herbert Simon and Allen Newell as well as the ELIZA that was developed by Joseph Weizenbaum. The ELIZA, which was made possible by funding provided by the RAND Corporation, had tremendous potential towards many goals like the interpretation of the machines that used spoken language and problem-solving capabilities. But still, it seemed like there was still plenty to get accomplished prior to the machines being able to think in a more abstract way, achieve the capability to process natural language, and to self-recognize.
Then came the 80s where we saw Artificial Intelligence research begin again as a whole new wave of tools and funding showed up. This timeframe was also when David Rumelhart and John Hopfield made the techniques of “deep learning” very popular as computers were now able to learn the commands they were taught by using their experience. However, it was Edward Feigenbaum who first introduced the expert systems that allowed the processes of decision making by a human to become mimicked. But still, it only came about during the 2000s where we saw a lot of the goals achieved as Artificial Intelligence thrived outside of the realm of needing public attention or funding from the government.
This is why today; the research into Artificial Intelligence is growing at an exponential rate and constant speed. During the past 5 years alone, the research into AI has jumped up over 12 percent a year globally.
Four years from now, China is expected to become a global force and the best source for AI. This advancement will easily take over the reins from the USA which has had a strong lead since 2004 as well as getting closer and closer to the overall lead that the European nations currently hold.
Because of Europe’s diverse and large region due to collaboration between countries, we will likely see them giving China a good run for their money in the Artificial Intelligence field. But never count any country out as far as the most research output is concerned. This is because India has a commanding lead in third and continues to show progress in their research.
The Most Common Advantages and Disadvantages of Artificial Intelligence
Due to the introduction of the computer, many manual processes have moved to the wayside or have been completely eliminated. With many computers being used just about everywhere now, this has created a strong dependence for them by humans.
As humans continue to surround themselves with the vast amount of computer systems and machines, humans have now begun to ask themselves “Are machines now able to behave and think like humans? Although this was only a thought, it obviously leads to the Artificial Intelligence that we now see today.
Advantages of Artificial Intelligence:
Artificial Intelligence’s nature has become very complex lately. Not only does it use a mix of complicated computer science, but it also mixes in a few other science types and mathematics. Due to these complexities, programming has helped the replication process of these computers to mimic the humans that built them.
Reduction in Error
Having AI continues to help us every day with error reduction. As well as increasing our chance of becoming accurate by using a more precise method. This can be seen in the many studies that have been conducted including space exploration.
We feed the intelligent robots with numerous amounts of information and then send them out to space to make sense of it all. Using machines for this purpose is great because of their ability to resist the harsh elements of space and be able to endure it for longer periods of time. Because of their design, they are acclimated so that they are unable to breakdown, get transformed, or become modified for other purposes.
Exploration is Difficult
Having Artificial Intelligence and the robotics that make it possible is a great tool to have when it comes to mining and other exploratory functions such as drilling for oil or natural gas. Besides that, having machines that are complex can allow us to explore more of the ocean’s depths like never before.
Having the programming available for the robots can put all of the laborious pressure put on them as they attain the responsibility. Also, they are very long-lasting.
Application is Daily
Perception, learning, and automated reasoning for computed methods have started to become common ground in today’s society. You can thank Cortana and Siri for that.
We are also continuing to hit the road by using GPS for those long drives we take. We also see that just about everybody has a smartphone so there is no doubt that we use AI in our daily lives. For utilities, we see that they are able to make predictions of what we might type and make spelling corrections that humans make. An intelligent machine does not get any simpler than that.
With picture taking, we see the AI detecting and identifying each face in the photo and tagging them via our social network profiles.
We employ Artificial Intelligence in the banking and financial sectors when we need to manage and organize important data. Being able to detect fraud uses an important type of AI developed for a system that is based on smart card technology.
Digital Assistants
Many organizations are currently using advanced technology that they label as “digital assistants”. These assistants are so advanced that they can interact with a human without having to be human themselves.
For digital assistants, their “emotions” are equivalent to thinking rationally, therefore, they will not be a distraction. When the emotions are removed completely, the assistants can logically think and make correct decisions.
Because emotion is connected to moods, judgment can be easily shrouded affecting efficiency. For Artificial Intelligence this is simply not the case.
Jobs are Repetitive
Many jobs that are considered repetitive or monotonous can easily be assigned to AI machines. Not only do machines think twice as fast as humans and multi-task faster. AI can also be tasked to work in dangerous situations. Unlike a human, their parameters are easily adjustable.
If you have ever played video games, then you have interacted with a type of Artificial Intelligence. When you are playing the video game by yourself, your opponent is the computer. You have noticed that your opponent seems to copy your moves based on your own moves. This is why gaming is the most popular form of AI technology.
Applications in Medicine
All throughout the field of medicine, there are many AI applications in use, as doctors make their assessments and determine their patients’ health risks by way of AI. The best way it assists them is by educating the patients concerning the medicine’s potential side effects.
Many medical staff members are trained in the use of AI by way of a simulator. The simulator uses an application that is big enough where many neuro disorders can be both monitored and detected because of its ability to simulate functions of the brain.
Many robotic applications are utilized when patients are struggling with mental health conditions like depression so that they are able to enjoy an active life.
An AI application that is popular in Oncology is a procedure called radiosurgery. It uses AI to remove tumors using radiation and without causing damage to any tissue surrounding the area.
No Break Time
Machines do not require any breaks to go to the bathroom or go to lunch. Their programming allows them to run for hours without getting tired, distracted, or bored with the work that they are doing.
Artificial Intelligence Disadvantages
Can be costly
To create AI, it requires a lot to go into it. This brings a high cost to the manufacturing of the technology. Plus, any maintenance or repair needed can also be costly.
The reason for the high cost is because of the special software that requires occasional upgrades. These upgrades will allow the technology to adapt to a new environment and become smarter as the AI technology advances. If serious issues occur, then the cost can be high just for the procedure involved to reinstate the AI or to retrieve codes.
No replication of humans
It is believed that intelligence is a pure gift. Because of this, an ongoing ethical battle rages on concerning the replication of human intelligence. In the case of Artificial Intelligence, there is no replication of humans. AI is only able to perform what it is programmed to do. It has no ability to judge or make decisions if given an unfamiliar situation. They can only perform the task correctly or incorrectly.
No experience to improve
Artificial Intelligence is unable to improve based on experience. It can only turn into wear and tear. Because of it storing a lot of data, the way the data is accessed is what matters when it comes to being different.
AI machines are not able to make changes to any response directed at an environment. This throws out the question of whether machines will truly replace humans.
Creativity is not original
Are you craving originality or creativity?
Well, you will not find it with AI. Though the technology is capable of creating or designing, they are in no way able to think or forge originality. With human beings, they are very emotional and sensitive creatures. They have many features that make them unique. They are guided by feelings and thoughts which machines completely lack.
How Artificial Intelligence Helps Business
The Artificial Intelligence of today has now become even more streamlined towards business. They are contributing a lot more in everyday endeavors that humans take part in. Today’s AI technology has its hands in many industries and is likely to make an explosive gain in the next decade.
E-commerce With a Personal Touch
It is estimated that about 80% of the interactions will be occurring only with an AI technology by the end of this decade. This means all of the comments made on social media, the chats, e-mails, and phone calls will be handled by some sort of automation system that replicates different functions of a human. AI today is gradually making changes in e-commerce in more ways than one. It is able to identify different patterns in the behavior of a customer after a purchase is made and by viewing credit checks. With the analyzation of these transaction types, Artificial Intelligence will be able to help pinpoint certain customers for certain offers. Doing this will make the customer feel like they are receiving personal attention from a business.
Another estimate figures that at least half of all leads generated for marketing are not followed up in a timely manner. This means that so much potential is slipping away. With AI, this would be easily prevented as potential customers become targeted customers. Plus, customers are being influenced by various amounts of media outlets today. By having AI integrated into a CRM, the business is able to create solutions that will reach the customer at the time they need it.
AI Assistance in Aviation
Many airlines continue to struggle tremendously to improve their interaction with customers while literally interacting with them on a daily basis through online markets. With AI, this would be the perfect place for it. With chatbots and other intelligent systems, they can be placed strategically to connect with passengers with concerns about flights, so they can be given notifications.
Business Automation is Incorporated
With many instances of AI present in automation, this has allowed a new revolution in business to occur. Everything from factory robots to hotel booking systems, the only thing that people don’t know is where the exact location of the AI is. As more and more work is becoming automated, AI is also automating several processes.
Cloud Databases Give Business Insight
Cloud databases are information-rich but are totally useless if it is not extracted and has the data analyzed. Having AI-enabled will assist in the search for the uniqueness that could be hidden in all of the data by processing the many data sets efficiently and quickly. The greatest part of it all is that the AI will adapt and learn quickly.
Communications Receive Chat-based Solutions
Many businesses today utilize chatbots that allow them to chat with customers without having a human behind it. An example is called Clickatell Touch. Clickatell uses technology comprised of a system called touch-card that combines online chat with interactions that are bot-driven with the automated business workflow.
Smarter Decisions Uncover Insights
Another purpose of an AI system is to be able to extract ideas from many different datasets, which can be very time-consuming or even hard to accomplish. A business that has CRM integrated with AI will be able to acquire this valuable data, thus being able to come up with timely ideas. Many businesses today are making decisions on the type of customer they should market towards and what the best possible offers are by using AI. Besides this, when AI is implemented, it will also help to identify leads that have the most potential.
Corporations Plan to Invest Heavily in AI
With data becoming even more cloud-based, it is also becoming less expensive and more accessible. With this low cost, some other perks include having an algorithm that can quickly sift through piles of data. This makes many corporations to want to get their hands on this technology. According to a 2016 Accenture survey, they found that at least 70% of corporations had planned to make a significant amount of AI investment.
Sales Efforts are Streamlined and Anticipated
An AI system can easily catch signals that sales executives usually miss because they are too busy looking through existing data. For example, when a support tool with AI integrated into it makes a prediction about a view that is based on interaction, that business can then use that information to establish relationships with customers. So, once a bad view happens, a company would be able to recognize it and turn it around into a good view by being proactive about the issues instead of waiting to hear it from a customer. If they hear it from a customer, then it may be too late to change their view of the company.
Interpreting Unlocked Unstructured Data
For structured data, which is made up of interpreted and analyzed data, it is accounted for only 15% of available information. The remaining data is considered unstructured and can come in handy if properly leveraged to attract new ideas. For example, if a utility company such as a gas company has plans to lie downpipe, there will be many questions beforehand. These questions will pertain to the history, previous structures, and any issues that arose previously. The training manuals will have these answers as well as old reports of inspection available on a shelf. This is how AI allows businesses to turn any unstructured data into intelligence that is actionable.
Basic Terminology in Artificial Intelligence
In physics, velocity is the speed of an object as it travels through space in a certain amount of time. When we think about it, artificial intelligence should not be much different. So below, we have the most widely used terms known in the AI field.
Artificial Intelligence
Understanding what AI is made up of is what we need to do first. The term itself is defined as being a computer science field that has a goal to create systems which are capable of solving problems and making decisions on their own based on given data.
General AI
This field is still emerging and is directed towards the making of intelligent machines. This means any systems used for general-purpose and has a good level of intelligence as compared to human intelligence. This is also referred to as “Strong Artificial Intelligence”.
Narrow Artificial Intelligence
This type of AI is the kind that is used to translate a language, play games like chess, and be able to recognize a face.
Super Artificial Intelligence
Considered to be 5 times smarter than the smartest human and can easily apply their knowledge to anything that is asked of it. This is the type of AI that would put Stephen Hawking or Albert Einstein to shame.
An Agent
This is also referred to as a bot, broker, or assistants. It is considered to be autonomous and uses sensors to make observations and actuators to interact with its environment.
Chatbot
A chatbot is an AI program that is capable of having human interaction via messaging. Their main goal is to imitate human behavior during a conversation.
Data
Data is any information that has been collected and is transformed into digital information.
Data Mining
Data mining is where patterns that have large data sets are found. The goal is to extract as much information as possible that is possible.
Deep Learning
Deep learning is a combination of Machine Learning and AI where layered neural networks get combined with the power of computing. They are also given a heaping handful of training data that will allow them to help in the creation of learning models that can process data in ways that can only be explained as exciting and new.
Genetic Algorithm
A genetic algorithm is used to solve problems of optimization by acting in the same way that natural selection acts. It selects random individuals in a population and pairs them together based on their compatibility to be parents.
Machine Learning
ML for short, it is an AI subset that allows the algorithms and programs to be taught a task and how to complete it by adding more effectiveness and efficiency as it continues to develop. Programs like these use the data from old performances for predicting and improving any future performances.
NLG
Natural language generation or NLG is a learning task where the algorithm makes an attempt at generating a language that sounds human and could be comprehended easily. Using natural language generation will allow the production of language that is computer-generated so there would be no difference in sound when compared to humans.
NLP
Natural language processing or NLP is what allows computers to understand human language and how to process it all to know what it all means. This involves a lot of interpretation of speech and text recognition.
Recurrent Neural Network
A Recurrent Neural Network or RNN is an artificial network consisting of data that is recorded and the outcomes get refed to create a cycle.
OCR
Optical Character Recognition or OCR is a system designed to take an image of different types of text and turns them into text that is readable.
Reinforcement Learning
Reinforcement learning is where the machine learns through different reward and experimentation processes. As it learns, it receives reinforcement that is positive every time the desired result occurs and reinforcement that is negative for every time when it does not.
Rule
A rule is a certain format that represents a knowledge base in the Expert System.
Supervised learning
Supervised learning uses input from a human to act as supervision. It is considered to be an important part of the machine’s process of continuous learning. You can easily compare it to how a teacher supervises a student.
Strong AI
Strong AI is a type of development that is designed to help all AI systems to be as skilled and useful like our minds.
Turing Test
This test was developed in 1950 by Alan Turing, which helps to make identifications concerning true AI. It surrounds a process that uses judgments that could recognize different interactions made with a human from the computer-generated interactions.
Unsupervised learning
Unsupervised learning is an algorithm type that attempts to create a conclusion from a dataset that is made up of different input data that does not use labeled responses.

Chapter 3: What is Deep Learning?
Deep learning is a specific technique used in machine learning that helps to teach computers to do natural human actions that are example-based. This makes it the driving force behind self-driving cars, teaching them how and when to stop, or how to recognize important objects and distinguish the differences. It is also the technology that is seen in all voice control devices including tablets, phones, TVs, and many hands-free devices. Lately, deep learning has been gaining a lot of ground by achieving unforeseen results.
Fundamental Concepts of Deep Learning, Artificial Neural Networks, Algorithms, Predictive Statistic, and Decision Trees
Deep Learning Fundamental Concepts
Logistic Regression
It is estimated that the analysis of regression stems from a relationship between different variables of statistical input that will allow a prediction concerning a variable. It is also a model used for variables for input that could also predict an outcome based on categories and can make up different values like no cancer-cancer, or for categorizing an image like car-bike-house-cat-dog-bird.
Logistic regression also applies to a function called logistic sigmoid where it weights the input values that help generate predictions of which two classes that the input data will belong to (or if a logistic regression is multinomial, what multiple class will it be).
This has similar properties of a perceptron that is non-linear or neural networks that do not have the layers. The difference, as compared to basic models, is that logistic regression is not hard to interpret and is very reliable when some of their statistical input values all hold. As long as the statistical properties all hold a reliable model, it can be produced with little to no data input. This is what will cause logistic regression to become a valuable asset when data is hard to come by, as we could imagine for the social and medical fields where the use of logistic regression is mainly used for the analyzation and interpretation of experimental results. Because of its fast and simple uses, we can see how it is used for data sets that are large in size.
Deep learning is also considered a part of a neural network for the purpose of classification and is often thought of as another form of logistic regression. As such, an algorithm for deep learning can easily be made up of multiple stages of learning, where they can pass along all of the features they have inside logistic regressions where inputs can be classified.
ANN
An Artificial Neural Network or ANN transforms some input data by calculating weighted sums over a few of the inputs, then applying a function that is not linear to the entire process to get an intermediate calculation. This three-step process is what we call a layer, where the function of the lesson is usually described as the unit. For the intermediate, they are normally termed as a feature and have the purpose of being the input in another layer.
With the repetition of the steps, an ANN is able to learn many layers that make up a non-linear layer, so it can then be combined to create a prediction from the last layer.
The way a neural network is able to learn is through the generation of error signals that easily measure differences of the desired values and network prediction and use that error signal to make weight adjustments to make accurate predictions.
Unit
The unit is referred to as a function for activation in the layers where the transformation of the inputs occurs. These usually take place by a nonlinear activation function. A unit also consists of many incoming and outgoing connections. Regardless of this, the units have been known to be complex and compared to units of LSTM, where an activation works using a specific layout of all of the connections to all of the activation functions of the nonlinear source. Called a maxout unit, these compute the overall output across a field of other input values. A convolution, pooling, and all other input functions have no reference to units.
Artificial Neurons
An artificial neuron can be implied as having a close neurobiology connection.
The term neuron was strongly encouraged during the time of minimal interest in Artificial Intelligence and was to be seen as a way to separate the failing perceptron from the neural network which was gaining ground. Unfortunately, because of the high amount of success that deep learning was achieving following 2012, the media outlets often made it sound misleading while comparing it to the brain of a human. Today, it is no longer coined as neuron and should be standard to label it as a unit.
Layer
The largest level reachable in deep learning is a layer. The layer is considered the container that receives all of the weighted input, changes it by using functions that are non-linear, then passes their values along as the output of the next layer. A layer is also mostly uniform as it normally contains only one function of activation (convolution, pooling, etc.) which lets it be compared easily to other network parts. As with all layers, the first is the input and the last layer is always the output while the hidden layers are those that lie in between.
Fundamental Concepts of Artificial Neural Networks
An ANN or Artificial Neuron Network is more popularly referred to as a Neural Network and is a model that is based on computational structures but operates similarly to the brains neurons. Their functions are so similar that it almost acts like an actual nervous system but only artificially for all of the processing, transmitting, and receiving all of the information.
In a neural network, there are only 3 layers that comprise it:
  1. The Input Layer (All inputs of the model get fed into it this way)
  2. The Hidden Layers (These layers are always consisting of many layers that do all of the processing of the inputs that are fed in)
  3. The Output Layer (This is the final data after processing is complete)
Below, we go in detail of these layers’ purposes.
The Input Layer
Starting it all is the Input Layer that conducts all of the communication to the outside environment and presents a good neural network pattern. Its sole purpose is to feed in the inputs. Once fed, they are relayed into the hidden layers. With the input layer, it should set the tone for the way the neural network’s training should be. That’s why the input layer must resemble a variable that is both independent and influential towards the output.
The Hidden Layer
This is the middle layer or (hidden layer) that contains all of the neurons and their functions for activation that is also applied to them all. As the middle layer, it is located between the input and output layer. Its sole purpose is designed around the processing of inputs that were fed to the input layer. So, the hidden layer is given more responsibility for the extraction of features obtained from all of the data that was inputted.
With a lot of research already put into the evaluation of the number of hidden layer neurons, there is still no exact result found yet. Plus, there is always the possibility of many hidden layers existing. So, this can lead you to believe that since there are many layers in the middle, then there will be many different problems that are specific to each. Let’s take for example if we have data that could be linearly segregated, then no hidden layers would be necessary due to the activation function being added to the input layer which would be allowed to solve problems. But if you have a problem that includes complex decisions, you can apply at least 5 of the hidden layers that are based on the amount of complexity or the amount of required accuracy. Remember though, this does not mean accuracy will be high just because of the layers being increased. A time will come when the amount of accuracy has been reached or will be constant after adding more layers.
Plus, the amount of neurons needs to be calculated in each of the networks. If that amount is less than the complexity of the problem, then the number of hidden layer neurons will also be less which could make them become inadequate to detect any signals in a data set that is complicated. If the amount of neurons exceeds the amount of network space, then an occurrence of overfitting may happen.
Output Layer
The third layer is the output layer of each neural network and it is responsible for the collection and transmission of all of the information as it is designed to do. The output layer’s pattern can also be traced back to where it came from, the input. The amount of neurons available in the output layer is usually related directly with the work that it is related to in the neural network. In order for the amount of output layer neurons to be determined, we must consider their intention within the network first.
Fundamental Concepts of Algorithms
Decomposition
Decomposition is a process where problems become manageable after being broken down. With programming, this entails having the algorithm broken down to create problems that are smaller so that they are able to be solved alone.
In larger programs, decomposition of problems continues until they are easy to be identified how to be written as individual subroutines in a program. This allows a team of developers to use this information because it is easily divided among them. So, for each problem that gets broken down, each subroutine can continue to be used over and over again for other similar problems.
Abstraction
With abstraction, we see all unnecessary detail is removed from the problems so that the focus can be on the details of the problems. This makes the problem solving a lot easier.
A good example of this is the map of the London Underground. You can clearly see all of the stations, the available services on them, and any other tube line details. This is what’s required when a passenger begins to plan their journey between stations. Details that are not included are the distances of stations, the number of platforms, their location, and how deep they are underground. They are excluded due to their irrelevance to the planning process.
Predictive Statistic
A form of computerized AI analysis is called predictive statistic and makes use of historical and current data, a status that is real-time, information based on performance, or algorithms that are able to make accurate predictions concerning the likely events of the future.
Many fields currently use predictive statistics. These include many businesses that are involved in disease-risk scoring in the healthcare field, for the prediction of no-shows to appointments in industries of high-value, or for fraud prevention and detection.
Using predictive statistics also have the following positives towards its use:
Competitive Advantage is Gained
As data is accessible by everybody, there are not a lot of businesses that effectively use the data so they can draw good insights concerning the behavior of the future. For the businesses that already have an advantage over the competition, this information can be used to produce service and products that are better customer-oriented, thus allowing them to win more in the market. This principle can also be used towards programs that give rewards and recognize loyalty from their customers.
Risk Reduction
Predictive statistics are greatly used in reducing the number of risks that occur in business. A lot of businesses that specialize in services and retail can take advantage of predictive statics to obtain ideas on how to make more of their products succeed or to have a better outlook on whom their products cater to. Many businesses that are service-based, for example, utility companies, will be able to use predictive statistics that will see the reduction of risks to downtime and any asset failure.
Cost Reduction
Predictive statistics can help bring cost lower by lowering risk. Plus, the future can be predicted a lot better as long as the amount of financial impact is not negative and is at a low level.
An example of this is when a business uses predictive statistics in determining the needs of maintaining assets prior to problems happening. They can then service the assets as they continue to function, allowing the prediction of asset maintenance. With predictive maintenance, we see the proactive addressing and prevention of problems, the increase of performance, and cost reduction that follows downtime and failures to assets.
Operational and Production Efficiency is Increased
Predictive statistics is a valuable tool when it comes to the identification of new optimized production, efficiencies in operations, and new opportunities to generate revenue. Predictive statistics can also be used by businesses to have the market assessed for new potential products. This will allow the needs of inventory forecasting achievable thus making production very efficient.
For businesses that are asset-based, the predictive statistics will permit a company to take the required steps to have their timelines and performance of assets to be optimized. This is in particular used by railway and logistics businesses as well as using predictive statistics in improving their schedules and timelines and also with the maintaining of vehicles used in their fleet.
Safety and Compliance Improvements
A business can use predictive statistics in fraud identification and in addressing various concerns in safety in utility and logistic workplaces. The obtained result is considered to be a level of higher compliance in the regulatory process as well as a reduced amount of fraud risk for the company.
Customers Wants are Understood
How many companies can say that they really know their customers? They may have general ideas of who their ideal and typical kind of customers are, but predictive statistics can lift the many insights to a higher level. They can also assist companies in building an in-depth and precise view of the customers that buy from them, the products that interest them, and what they may want to buy later on.
So, a company may realize that their main customers make up those in their twenties, but what they may not realize is that their biggest opportunity of keeping and continuing to win the customers will begin with those in their early twenties and who are more poised to become an entrepreneur so that they can create and grow a business. This gives an opportunity to produce products that can lead to success and greater improvement of retention and overall profits.
When predictive statistics are leveraged, a business can begin to tailor their offerings to their customers’ future desires.
Data is Instantly Processed
There is no doubt that as soon as IoT and AI become more and more widely used, a company’s collection of data will be significantly increased. This will make the processing of data harder to accomplish.
Predictive statistics uses an AI algorithm that is able to process the data at a faster rate but also maintaining effectiveness and using insights in a less amount of time. So, the more collected data that you have, then your analytics will be accurate.
Decision Trees
A decision tree is a supervised learning method that is non-parametric and used in regression and classification. The learning method of a decision tree stems from data that approximates a curve called a sine curve that comprises of rules called if-then-else decisions. So, as the decision tree becomes deeper, the decision rules become more complex and the model becomes fitter.
Basic Terminology in Deep Learning
With Deep Learning being a fairly new form of Machine Learning, it continues to grow and receive a vast amount of interest throughout the media. This can all be thanked in part by its role in the field of AI and its many applications that include self-driving cars, image recognition, and the matches of Lee Sedol vs. AlphaGo.
More recently, the techniques of Deep Learning are becoming more popular with the solving of problems that involve NLP as we see with Sentiment Analysis.
Below, we have listed a few simple Deep Learning terms that will provide you with a basic and simple understanding. Hopefully, you will be able to take what you read and apply it just as easy as you read them.
Perceptron
A perceptron is recognized as the equivalent to a brain neuron that is able to transmit and process all information on a more simplified level. A perceptron does this by taking many inputs and weighing them so that a single output can be produced. Each of the input’s weight is based on how important the decision of the output is.
Artificial Neural Networks
An Artificial Neural Network or ANN for short is a model that is influenced by different neural networks of biological origin like the CNS of all creatures.
The ANN is a device used for processing just like physical hardware or algorithms, where they are modeled against a mammal’s brain although on a much smaller size.
Backpropagation
Backpropagation is an algorithm that trains and teaches the neural network. For the neural network to be trained, the input is given to it to produce an output.
First, you must teach a neural network the right way to achieve the desired output and the way it should have been if the output is not the desired outcome. Then the ANN takes this output and adapts it to the weights so that they enhance the output’s yield and precision. This is repeated over and over until the error rate is at a more acceptable level.
Convolutional Neural Networks
A CNN or convolutional neural network is a type of neural network that employs many replicas of an identical neuron. A benefit of this is that a network becomes enabled to learn from a neuron one time and then uses that in many other places to help make the model simplified while learning which will reduce the amount of error. This is how CNN's have become useful when it comes to image tagging and recognition of objects. A CNN is able to learn an inputs abstract representation after a convolution. When it comes to recognizing an object, CNNs start out as data that has raw pixels. It learns features that are discriminative like the edges, and then basic shapes, textures, patterns, and complex shapes follow.
Recurrent Neural Network
A Recurrent Neural Networks or RNN utilizes the sequential information. As compared to neural networks of traditional classes, an assumption is made concerning the inputs and outputs that they are considered to be independent of each other. An RNN is also reliant on the computations that occurred prior to the current state. This can make the RNNs seem like a neural network that is gradually unrolled. An entity’s RNN connection creates a cycle that is directed, thus creating a type of memory that is internal which then helps leverage the model over many dependent chains.
Recursive Neural Network
The Recursive Neural Network is a concept based on the Recurrent Neural Network which comes about by connecting a consistent and fixed amount of recursive or repetitive weights over a structure. A Recursive Neural Network has many features of a tree, but a Recurrent Neural Network represents a chain. A Recursive Neural Network has also been a part of NLP when needed for a Sentiment Analysis task.
Supervised Neural Network
For an ideal output to be produced by an SNN, it must have some previous relation to the output. This is how it gets trained via a dataset that is pre-defined and is also based on the current dataset, which allows outputs that are accurate to be created. This could ultimately be viewed as being supervised as it learns, by giving the answer to the question.
Unsupervised Neural Network
A UNN involves a machine being provided data sets that are unlabeled and have never been trained to use before, which has the goal of discovering various trends and patterns through the process of clustering.
Gradient Descent
An algorithm called Gradient Descent is used in determining a functions local minimum. This is achieved by guessing a solution and applying the function’s gradient at that time. We then guide each solution in the opposite direction that the gradient is at and repeat it up to the point where the algorithm becomes converged with the gradient at the local minimum of zero.
Word Embedding
Word embedding has similarities to what a painting has as if representing a person, which in this case, word embedding is representative of words that use numbers of real value. A word embedding can also be trained as well as used to obtain some similar features between the relations and words. These number arrangements represent both syntactic and semantic word information that is in easy formats that can be understood by computers.

Chapter 4: What is Data Science?
When we are talking about Data Science, we are mentioning the principles, algorithms, and many other tools that have the same goal of discovering patterns that were hidden among raw data.
So, what is the difference between this and the data discovered by statisticians?
The possible answer could be easily found among the difference of predicting and explaining.
From a data analyst’s point of view, they are usually seen explaining things that are going on as they process a data’s history. The other side of this, a data scientist uses a variety of MLAs that are advanced enough to be used alongside an exploratory analysis that can discover various insights into an event.
With data science, it’s primary use is to come up with predictions and decisions by way of causal analytics that is predictive, machine learning, and prescriptive analytics (a combination of decision and predictive science).
Introduction of the fundamental concepts of data science and its applications
The fundamental concepts of data science include the importance of data science in business and its application. First, you must have an understanding of regression analysis and how the mining process for data sets work, plus the algorithms and tools that will be used daily so one can master the discipline.
Basic Python Programming
Basic Python Programming is a great way to receive an introduction to statistics via application as well as learning about data science. Python presents the basic skill that this field requires to understand it. But, before you can start working with any data, a firm understanding of how it is extracted must also be known. This is how Python is able to represent itself as the basic tool needed for refining and manipulating it all.
From the very first course you take, you should be taught the basic fundamentals for the Python program so that you have a clear understanding of the CVS files and the best way through the many data structures. These core fundamentals include the understanding of querying, t-tests, the process of tabular data, and distributions and sampling.
SQL and Databases
All data that we see today is mostly mined out of databases that have a minimal amount existing in structured forms. An SQL or “Structured Query Language” is a powerful language that is used to communicate with a database so we can understand them, extract the rich data that they hold, and explore every aspect of them to ensure you have the right data for your current problem. Being able to work with SQL, creating instances of databases via the cloud, running queries of SQL, and accessing the many data sets and databases in the real world is a very necessary skill that every data scientist should have.
The Bayesian Approach and Statistics
A person must have some knowledge of statistics in order to fully understand data science. Because statistics being such a broad field, a data scientist must be able to grasp a few concepts of probability theory in order to provide insights that are practical to a company.
This theory needs to be combined with a lot of practice by way of learning the core fundamentals like regression, testing, distribution, the theory of Bayesian probability, and hypothesis. With most ML modules, they are all mostly based on the Bayesian probability models. With the Bayesian, the approach is considered intuitive in that it goes from the probability straight over to data analysis which allows better accounting for uncertainty and for the providing assumptions and statements that are actionable and are practiced.
Techniques of Algorithmic programming
In order to master the field of data science, you must be able to learn the many ways of solving problems using the algorithmic techniques available. When an algorithm is used, you are able to manipulate the data stored in the data structures. You also need to learn the way that these structures get implemented throughout the various languages in programming, the best way to break down the big problems into smaller ones, and what to expect in return. A lot of strategies are available to learn and design algorithms. These include learning to recursively solve problems, balancing a binary tree, and how a dynamic array gets resized.
How Data Science Helps Business
In business, power is attained through knowledge, and the fuel that the power is created from is the data. Estimates have been made that by the year 2020, the amount of data volume in gigabytes will be more than 45 trillion. So being able to grab a hold of all this data using data science would be a valuable asset to have. With data science, it uses several methods, algorithms, systems, and processes in order to have knowledge extracted from the data available and then having this data leveraged in order to have decisions made.
If you take an approach that is analytical with numbers, statistics, and facts, then you will have a solution that is reasonable and not seemed to be apparent. Due to the insights that are offered from data science, many companies have begun to utilize data science for the decisions that are evidence-based, to better understand customers and promote the training of employees. When investing in data science, you will begin to see a tremendous amount of value increased right from the start in five ways:
Quantifiable Evidence Promotes Better Decision Making
Data must always be available in order for a company to make a decision. Although this is never easy to do because the majority of the data is considered to be unstructured and requires analytic tools to be able to receive ideas based on the data. When the statistics and numbers are pulled through the use of data science, a company can start to create models that can predict and simulate many possibilities.
Along with that, companies will be able to learn the right solution for them to take so they receive the best outcome possible and having logical, best-case scenarios prescribed in order to have performance improved. Also, by having performance metrics analyzed and recorded, a company will be efficient and a lot smarter at decision-making that is based on trends.
Relevant Product Improvement
Using the methodologies of data science will allow you to analyze the market, make competitive comparisons, and explore some historicals of your services or products to determine when the best time to sell them will be. This will help a business to understand their products’ function and the way others will be helped by it.
With a constant reflection and analysis that is achieved by data science, it will provide an understanding of the response caused by the market and a company’s services and products. As a hard look is taken at a product and the way it is mostly used, you will be able to rethink the way the model you use is offering services that would benefit your customers the most.
Best Talent is Recruited
It can be an exhausting task to recruit the best talent available. But with the help of data science, the recruiting process will be more accurate, and much faster. With the vast amount of available data points towards talent because of databases, job sites, and social media, many businesses can get through these data points by using methods that are analytical to pin-point the best available candidates that meet the qualifications of the job.
When this data is mined on applicants, you are more confident in knowing that the applicants will fit in like family and not only look good in a resume. This plays more importantly in times where you are looking to fill a vacancy very rapidly. Being able to work smarter by using data science will assure you that the vacancy will have the best applicant filling it.
Staff Training
Regardless of having the right kind of staff, it can be daunting as you try to keep the staff updated and informed of company happenings. The use of data science allows you to grab ideas that could be important to your employees. The knowledge that is gained is then inputted into software designed for online knowledge where employees can then access it as the information becomes relevant to their job. As hard data is pulled, it can also provide facts and statistics for all employees. This will ultimately create a team that will have the knowledge to drive the business forward.
Obtaining a Target Audience
It is believed that the amount of data created daily is around 3 billion BGs. With a large amount of data like this, it can seem impossible to collect the most important customer information. For all of the data collected from a customer, it all can be easily analyzed to obtain a better understanding of customers.
When data science is used for the information obtained by customers, you will be able to combine all data points in order to gain insights that will target a specific audience a lot more. This will allow you to provide tailored products and service to groups. These groups can be based on age or income level where the company could then strategize to create certain types of promotions that tailor to those groups. When data science methodologies are implemented in a company, a value can be added instantly through training, recruiting, marketing, and decision making. Being able to analyze these areas will ensure company growth through ways that are strategic.
Data Science in the Real World
In the real world, data science has changed in a tremendous way and has, in some ways, revolutionized different ways for a business to function more efficiently.
Today, it seems like our daily lives are connected to a gadget in one way or another, with a huge amount of data that also seems to emanate from different sources. Because of this, we seem to be faced with challenges in the study and analysis of a high amount of data if we only use traditional tools for data processing. To get past the challenges, there are some solutions available like Hadoop. Many more companies are maximizing the benefits of the data. Companies are now finding out that they too can benefit in a way that will help their growth. There are more and more opportunity’s being created in different sectors. Let us take a look at a few of the sectors where data science is playing a good role.
Data Science in the Education Industry
There is a huge flood of information and data streaming from the education system. Although this data solely relates to student information, it also includes data concerning the results, courses, and faculty. Having this data allows us to analyze it in order to gain important insight that will be used to increase the overall effectiveness of educational institutions.
The following are a few fields in education that either have been or can be improved following the analyzing of data.
  • Learning Programs that are Customized and Dynamic
Having customized programs that are dynamic will benefit many students and are created by using the collected student data showcasing their learning history. This will allow individual results to improve.
  • Course Material Reframed
Having course material reframed in accordance with all the collected data and based on student learning and monitoring in real-time of a course would be very beneficial to students.
  • Grading Systems
Many grading systems have been either introduced or advanced due to the analyzing of data.
  • Prediction of Careers
Having an analysis of student records will let us see the interests, progress, weaknesses, and strengths. It also helps in determining what the most appropriate career choice would be for the student after graduation.
Data Science in Healthcare
The healthcare industry is packed full of data that concerns all types of information pertaining to patient care.
  • The data increases cost reduction for various treatments due to lessening the amounts of diagnosis.
  • The data can be used to predict any outbreak or possible epidemic and also help in the decision of what proper measures should be taken.
  • The data can help to avoid all preventable diseases from occurring by using early detection. This will allow for early treatment and prevent conditions from getting worse.
  • Data can help in deciding which medicine is best to be prescribed based on evidence and research on previous results.
Example
There have been many introductions of wearable sensors and devices to the healthcare field and are able to provide feeds in real-time directly to the patient’s record. A technology like this is currently available from Apple.
Apple currently provides 3 types of technology for the healthcare field which are the Apple ResearchKit, HealthKit, and CareKit. The goal of these kits is to allow iPhone customers to upload their record information via their phone.
Data Science in Government
No matter which country’s government you call yours, there will always be a ton of data available to look at. The reason why is because the governments need to keep track of their citizens by using different types of databases and records including vital statistics, geographics and surveys, and population growth.
All of this data is important, and with the proper analysis and study, can help a government tremendously in the following ways:
Welfare Services
  • Decisions are made faster concerning different social and welfare programs.
  • Areas are identified as having immediate attention needed.
  • Monitoring agriculture and keeping updated data concerning livestock and agricultural land.
  • Being able to withstand challenges and decrease the risk of energy exploitation, terrorism, and unemployment.
Cyber Security
  • Data science plays a major role in detecting and recognizing deceit.
  • It can be implemented to detect tax evasion.
Example
The FDA, under federal guidelines, benefits from data science by analyzing data that could detect patterns so that food sources can be monitored efficiently in case of mass food contamination.
Data Science in Media and Entertainment
With many people owning at least one piece of digital technology, a ton of data can only occur because of it. This makes it the number one reason for the increase in data.
Besides that, many of the social media outlets provide an alternate way to collect data. Many businesses in these industries have figured out the importance of this data, so benefiting from it has become second nature.
Benefits of science data in media and entertainment
  • Audience interests are predicted efficiently.
  • All media streams are optimized for distribution through media and digital platforms.
  • Insights are obtained through reviews.
  • Advertisements effectively target the consumer.
Example
The on-demand music giant, Spotify, uses data science and analytics by collecting subscriber data. Then after analyzing the data, they are able to create recommendations to the individual users.
Data Science in Weather Patterns
Various weather satellites and sensors are currently orbiting the world. That means a lot of weather data is being collected on a daily basis. This data is then used for monitoring all weather conditions.
The data that gets collected is due to data science playing a big role in the following ways:
  • Accurate weather predictions
  • Studying and monitoring of global warming
  • Monitoring and analyzing natural disaster patterns
  • Developing emergency preparedness plans
  • Predicting when usable water is available for drought areas

Data Science in Transportation
Ever since data science has been in use, it has been very detrimental throughout the transportation industry. Not only has it made it efficient, but have also played a major role in the following ways:
  • Planning of routes
Data science is used in the planning of routes based on the needs of users that are taking certain routes or using different modes of travel.
  • Management of Congestion and traffic control
The use of data science can provide a real-time prediction of traffic congestion as well as the times of day when congestion will be worst. We see this already in use via the use of Google Maps.
  • Amount of Traffic Safety
Data science provides real-time analysis to determine areas that are more prone to accidents. This will ultimately reduce the number of accidents while increasing safety.
Example
A good example of this is Uber. Uber utilizes a ton of data regarding its drivers, locations, vehicles, and trip data. This data then gets analyzed and is used to measure supply and demand, locate drivers, and calculate future fare prices.
Data Science in Banking
The banking and finance field is booming with data and is predicted to grow by at least 200 percent by 2020. Having good analysis will help in the detection of fraud and all other activities concerning money like:
  • Credit/debit card fraud
  • Counterfeiting
  • Business clarity
  • Alteration of customer statistics
  • Money laundering
  • Risk mitigation
Example
There are many types of software used to deter money laundering including one called SAS AML that uses data analytics to monitor transactions that seem suspicious and then analyzing the customer data.
Introduction of: Data Analytics, Big Data, Data Mining, Computer Science and its Applications in Business
Data Analytics
Data analytics, or DA for short, is a method used to examine multiple sets of data to be able to form a conclusion generated out of their information. The techniques and technologies of data analytics are used widely among the commercial industry so that companies can then make informed decisions based on research so that it can be either verified or disproved as an effective model, hypothesis, or theory.
Inside the data analytics process
The process of analytics involves a process of data collection where a data scientist will help to identify information needed for an application of analytics, then when done, they go and work on the application alone or together with a data engineer. If data comes from a system of different source, then it may have to be combined using a data integration routine that gets transformed through common formats and then loaded inside of an analytics system like a Hadoop, data warehouse, or a NoSQL database. For other purposes, the process of collection may include pulling of subsets that are relevant from a stream of data that is raw and flows into a Hadoop and then moves to a partition that is separate from the system, so it will be easily analyzed without the data set being affected overall.
Big Data
To understand what big data is, it is good to know some of its background histories. So, according to the definition found in Gartner (which is still the most current definition): Big data is a kind of data that has a wider variety of data that shows up in great amounts and an even fiercer amount of velocity.
Simply put, this big data is 5 times larger and has data sets that have a higher amount of complexity that stems from newly formed sources of data. With these new sets of data, they are so jam-packed with data that the software used for traditional processing of data is simply unable to manage it all. The good thing is that the amount of data can address problems in business that would not have been addressed beforehand.
Big Data’s History
The original idea for big data is still fairly new, although big data’s main origins stem as far back as the ‘60s while data was just getting created and organized in the very first data center along with a rational database becoming developed.
In 2005, it was starting to be realized that a large amount of data was being generated through the social networks of YouTube, Twitter, and Facebook. This was the same year that Hadoop (a framework that is open-sourced to analyze and store huge data sets) became developed. We also saw NoSQL become popular at this point, too.
When these frameworks for open-source were developed, they became essential for big data’s growth due to their ability to make storage cheaper and work easier. In the years to follow, big data did nothing but skyrocket. With many users continuing to generate this data, it seems to have no end in sight. But, humans are not the only ones who are generating this big data. When the Internet of Things was created, many devices and objects got connected and immediately began to gather big data concerning the usage pattern of customers and the products overall performance. With the introduction of machine learning, the amount of data produced continues to grow.
Big data has come a long way, and the full use of it is only at its infancy. With the expansion of computing being generated in the Cloud, this creates even more possibilities for big data to go farther. With the Cloud being scalable, developers can easily create clusters of ad hoc if they want to test data subsets.
Data Mining
With data mining, we are using a process that analyzes a data’s hidden pattern by using various perspectives of categorization of information that is useful, assembled, and collected in a common area, like a warehouse for data, to be analyzed efficiently, algorithms for data mining, business decision-making facilitation, and various other requirements that will finally conduct cost-cutting and have revenue increased.
It is known also as knowledge discovery or data discovery.
There are four major steps that are involved in the process of data mining and they are:
  • The extraction, transforming, and loading of data into big data warehouses.
  • Managing and storing of data in Cloud databases.
  • Access of data is provided to analysts via a software application.
  • Data that is analyzed is presented in an easy to understand way.
Data mining begins by gathering data that is both relevant and business-critical. Data that concerns a company is considered to be metadata, non-operational, or transactional. With transactional data, this is the daily dealings of the business like cost, inventory, and sales. Data that is non-operational is considered forecast, and metadata involves a database’s logical design. Data elements containing relationships and patterns are then able to render information that is relevant, thus increasing the revenue of an organization. When a business has a strong focus on consumers by using the techniques of data mining, they are able to provide a visualization of the products that were sold, the product’s price, the demographics of the customer, and competition information.
An example of this is WalMart. This giant retailer is able to transmit relevant information to a big data warehouse using terabytes. It can then be accessed easily by a supplier that can enable them the power to identify the purchasing patterns of a customer. These shopping patterns involve the most days shopped, most products sought after as well as other data that data mining can recognize.
Second, an algorithm that is suitable for data mining must be selected. This algorithm must then be able to identify trends in a data set and use the output to define a parameter. With the algorithms that are more popular, they are used for relationship identification in the elements of the data. With vendors like SQL and Oracle, the data mining incorporates an algorithm like a regression tree or clustering so that the data mine’s demand is met.

So much so, that anybody who has a computer science degree can easily land a job in just about any field. You can even collaborate with customers to develop content that is customized to their needs.

Today, it seems like everybody wants to look available while being online. This explains why the field of web development is so popular. Not only can a company develop, maintain, and upload websites for other companies, but they can also personalize them for individual clientele. If you plan on expanding you can easily upgrade to utilize SEO and other tactics that improve an online presence.

As more computers and electronic gadgets get used on a daily basis, they will also need more support in maintaining their equipment. While several companies hire staff made up of IT professionals, a smaller business will most likely use outsourcing. Marketing
